\paragraph{How to read this document.} Section~\ref{sec:func} writes the whole network as one composed function. Section~\ref{sec:algo} gives readable pseudocode for the forward pass and for every distinct block type. Section~\ref{sec:compact} lists the weights of each block type \emph{once}, with how many times it repeats. Section~\ref{sec:gloss} explains in plain language what every kind of layer does. The original layer-by-layer table with every repetition written out is in Appendix~\ref{app:full}.
\subsection*{Notation}
\begin{tabular}{ll}
\toprule
$B$, $T$ & batch size, sequence length (tokens) \\
$d$ & hidden size (3840) \\
$V$ & vocabulary size (262,208) \\
$H,\ H_{kv},\ d_h$ & query heads, key/value heads, size of one head \\
$W_\ast,\ b_\ast$ & learned weight matrix / bias of the named projection \\
$\odot$, $\sigma$ & element-wise product, logistic sigmoid \\
$f^{\circ n}$ & $f$ applied $n$ times in a row ($f\circ f\circ\cdots\circ f$) \\
$g \circ f$ & first $f$, then $g$ (composition reads right to left) \\
$\op{RMSNorm}(x)$ & $\gamma \odot x / \sqrt{\mathrm{mean}(x^2)+\epsilon}$ (learned scale $\gamma$) \\
$\op{LN}(x)$ & LayerNorm: $\gamma\odot(x-\mu)/\sigma + \beta$ \\
$\op{RoPE}$ & rotary position embedding (rotates query/key pairs by an angle set by position) \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\section{The Model as a Function}\label{sec:func}
Input: token ids $x \in \{0,\dots,V{-}1\}^{B\times T}$. Output: next-token logits $y \in \mathbb{R}^{B\times T\times V}$. The 48 decoder layers are written with composition powers, so a block that repeats $n$ times appears once as $\mathcal{B}^{\circ n}$.
\begin{align*}
  h_0 &= \op{Merge}\Big(\op{Embed}(x)\cdot\sqrt{d},\ \op{Proj}_{\mathrm{img}}\big(\op{Enc_V}(\mathrm{img})\big)\Big) \\
  h_L &= \Big(\big(\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{F}} \circ \mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{S}}^{\circ 5}\big)^{\circ 8}\Big)(h_0) \\
  y &= W_{E}^{\top}\,\op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{final}}(h_L)
\end{align*}
$\op{Merge}$ puts the projected image/audio vectors at the placeholder-token positions of the text embedding sequence. The encoders are themselves repeated stacks:
\begin{align*}
  \op{Enc_V} &= \op{Post}\circ \mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\circ 27}\circ \op{PatchEmbed}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Language-model decoder (48 layers).} 
layers 0--47: [$\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{S}}$$\times5$, $\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{F}}$] repeated 8$\times$.
\paragraph{Vision encoder (27 layers).} 
layers 0--26: [$\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{V}}$] repeated 27$\times$.
\subsection{Block functions}
\paragraph{$\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{S}}$ --- Sliding-window attention block.}
\begin{align*}
  a &= h + \op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{pa}}\big(\op{Attn}(\op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{in}}(h))\big) && \text{\footnotesize norm before AND after attention} \\
  \mathcal{B}(h) &= a + \op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{pf}}\big(\op{FFN}(\op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{pre}}(a))\big) && \text{\footnotesize norm before AND after the MLP} \\
  Q &= W_Q\,u && \text{\footnotesize $H=16$ heads of size $d_h=256$} \\
  K,\ V &= W_K\,u,\quad W_V\,u && \text{\footnotesize $H_{kv}=8$ key/value heads} \\
  Q,\ K &= \op{RMSNorm}_q(Q),\ \op{RMSNorm}_k(K) && \text{\footnotesize per-head normalisation} \\
  Q,\ K &= \op{RoPE}(Q),\ \op{RoPE}(K) && \text{\footnotesize rotate by position} \\
  S_h &= s\, Q_h K_{g(h)}^{\top} + M && \text{\footnotesize mask $M$: causal + sliding window of $w=1024$} \\
  P_h &= \op{softmax}(S_h) && \text{\footnotesize attention weights} \\
  O_h &= P_h\, V_{g(h)} \\
  \op{Attn}(u) &= W_O\,[O_1;\dots;O_H] \\
  \op{FFN}(u) &= W_{\mathrm{down}}\big(\op{GELU}_{\tanh}(W_{\mathrm{gate}}\,u)\odot W_{\mathrm{up}}\,u\big) && \text{\footnotesize gated MLP, width 15360}
\end{align*}
\paragraph{$\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{F}}$ --- Full (global) attention block.}
\begin{align*}
  a &= h + \op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{pa}}\big(\op{Attn}(\op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{in}}(h))\big) && \text{\footnotesize norm before AND after attention} \\
  \mathcal{B}(h) &= a + \op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{pf}}\big(\op{FFN}(\op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{pre}}(a))\big) && \text{\footnotesize norm before AND after the MLP} \\
  Q &= W_Q\,u && \text{\footnotesize $H=16$ heads of size $d_h=256$} \\
  K,\ V &= W_K\,u,\quad W_V\,u && \text{\footnotesize $H_{kv}=8$ key/value heads} \\
  Q,\ K &= \op{RMSNorm}_q(Q),\ \op{RMSNorm}_k(K) && \text{\footnotesize per-head normalisation} \\
  Q,\ K &= \op{RoPE}(Q),\ \op{RoPE}(K) && \text{\footnotesize rotate by position} \\
  S_h &= s\, Q_h K_{g(h)}^{\top} + M && \text{\footnotesize mask $M$: causal} \\
  P_h &= \op{softmax}(S_h) && \text{\footnotesize attention weights} \\
  O_h &= P_h\, V_{g(h)} \\
  \op{Attn}(u) &= W_O\,[O_1;\dots;O_H] \\
  \op{FFN}(u) &= W_{\mathrm{down}}\big(\op{GELU}_{\tanh}(W_{\mathrm{gate}}\,u)\odot W_{\mathrm{up}}\,u\big) && \text{\footnotesize gated MLP, width 15360}
\end{align*}
\paragraph{$\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{V}}$ --- Vision encoder block.}
\begin{align*}
  a &= h + \op{Attn}(\op{LN}_{1}(h)) && \text{\footnotesize pre-norm + residual} \\
  \mathcal{B}(h) &= a + \op{FFN}(\op{LN}_{2}(a)) && \text{\footnotesize pre-norm + residual} \\
  Q &= W_Q\,u + b_{Q} && \text{\footnotesize $H=16$ heads of size $d_h=72$} \\
  K,\ V &= W_K\,u + b_{K},\quad W_V\,u + b_{V} && \text{\footnotesize $H_{kv}=16$ key/value heads} \\
  S_h &= s\, Q_h K_h^{\top} + M && \text{\footnotesize mask $M$: bidirectional} \\
  P_h &= \op{softmax}(S_h) && \text{\footnotesize attention weights} \\
  O_h &= P_h\, V_h \\
  \op{Attn}(u) &= W_O\,[O_1;\dots;O_H] + b_O \\
  \op{FFN}(u) &= W_2\,\op{GELU}_{\tanh}(W_1 u + b_1) + b_2 && \text{\footnotesize MLP, width 4304}
\end{align*}
\section{Forward Pass in Pseudocode}\label{sec:algo}
\begin{algorithm}[H]
\caption{gemma-3-12b-it: full forward pass}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\Require token ids $x$ of shape $B\times T$; optional images / audio
\State $h \gets \op{Embed}(x)\cdot\sqrt{d}$ \Comment{look up one vector per token: $B{\times}T{\times}3840$}
\If{image input present}
\State $e \gets \op{Enc_V}(\text{image})$ \Comment{vision encoder: 27 layers, Algorithm~\ref{alg:vision}}
\State $h[\text{image token slots}] \gets \op{Proj}(e)$ \Comment{project into text width 3840}
\EndIf
\For{$r \gets 0$ \textbf{to} $7$} \Comment{layers 0--47: pattern of 6 repeated 8 times}
\For{$j \gets 1$ \textbf{to} $5$}
\State $h \gets \mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{S}}(h)$ \Comment{Sliding-window attention block}
\EndFor
\State $h \gets \mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{F}}(h)$ \Comment{Full (global) attention block}
\EndFor
\State $h \gets \op{RMSNorm}_{\mathrm{final}}(h)$
\State $y \gets h\,W_E^{\top}$ \Comment{logits $B{\times}T{\times}262,208$, embedding matrix re-used}
\State \Return $y$ \Comment{softmax$(y)$ = next-token probabilities}
\end{algorithmic}
\end{algorithm}
\begin{algorithm}[H]
\caption{Vision encoder}\label{alg:vision}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\State $h \gets \op{PatchEmbed}(\text{pixels}) + \text{position embedding}$ \Comment{one vector per image patch}
\For{$\ell \gets 0$ \textbf{to} $26$}
\State $h \gets \mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{V}}(h)$
\EndFor
\State $h \gets \op{Post}(h)$ \Comment{final norm / pooling / merging, see Table~\ref{tab:nonrep}}
\State \Return $h$
\end{algorithmic}
\end{algorithm}
\begin{algorithm}[H]
\caption{Block $\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{S}}(h)$ --- Sliding-window attention block}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\Require $h$ of shape $B\times T\times 3840$
\State $r \gets h$ \Comment{keep the input for the residual (skip) connection}
\State $u \gets \op{RMSNorm}(h)$ \Comment{normalise every token vector}
\Statex \hspace{-1.2em}\textit{token mixing: multi-head self-attention}
\State $Q \gets u W_Q^{\top}$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}256$ (16 query heads)}
\State $K \gets u W_K^{\top};\ V \gets u W_V^{\top}$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}8{\times}256$ (8 key/value heads, each shared by 2 query heads)}
\State $Q \gets \op{RMSNorm}(Q);\ K \gets \op{RMSNorm}(K)$ \Comment{normalise each head vector (stabilises training)}
\State $Q, K \gets \op{RoPE}(Q, K)$ \Comment{encode position by rotating vector pairs}
\State $P \gets \op{softmax}(s\, Q K^{\top} + M)$ \Comment{mask: causal + only the last 1024 tokens}
\State $O \gets P V$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}256$}
\State $m \gets O\, W_O^{\top}$ \Comment{concatenate heads, project back to $B{\times}T{\times}3840$}
\State $h \gets r + \op{RMSNorm}(m)$ \Comment{post-attention norm, then add residual}
\Statex \hspace{-1.2em}\textit{channel mixing: feed-forward network (each token independently)}
\State $r \gets h;\ u \gets \op{RMSNorm}(h)$
\State $m \gets \big(\op{GELU}_{\tanh}(u W_{\mathrm{gate}}^{\top}) \odot u W_{\mathrm{up}}^{\top}\big) W_{\mathrm{down}}^{\top}$ \Comment{expand to 15360, gate, shrink back to 3840}
\State $h \gets r + \op{RMSNorm}(m)$ \Comment{post-feed-forward norm, then add residual}
\State \Return $h$
\end{algorithmic}
\end{algorithm}
\begin{algorithm}[H]
\caption{Block $\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{F}}(h)$ --- Full (global) attention block}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\Require $h$ of shape $B\times T\times 3840$
\State $r \gets h$ \Comment{keep the input for the residual (skip) connection}
\State $u \gets \op{RMSNorm}(h)$ \Comment{normalise every token vector}
\Statex \hspace{-1.2em}\textit{token mixing: multi-head self-attention}
\State $Q \gets u W_Q^{\top}$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}256$ (16 query heads)}
\State $K \gets u W_K^{\top};\ V \gets u W_V^{\top}$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}8{\times}256$ (8 key/value heads, each shared by 2 query heads)}
\State $Q \gets \op{RMSNorm}(Q);\ K \gets \op{RMSNorm}(K)$ \Comment{normalise each head vector (stabilises training)}
\State $Q, K \gets \op{RoPE}(Q, K)$ \Comment{encode position by rotating vector pairs}
\State $P \gets \op{softmax}(s\, Q K^{\top} + M)$ \Comment{mask: causal: a token sees only earlier tokens}
\State $O \gets P V$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}256$}
\State $m \gets O\, W_O^{\top}$ \Comment{concatenate heads, project back to $B{\times}T{\times}3840$}
\State $h \gets r + \op{RMSNorm}(m)$ \Comment{post-attention norm, then add residual}
\Statex \hspace{-1.2em}\textit{channel mixing: feed-forward network (each token independently)}
\State $r \gets h;\ u \gets \op{RMSNorm}(h)$
\State $m \gets \big(\op{GELU}_{\tanh}(u W_{\mathrm{gate}}^{\top}) \odot u W_{\mathrm{up}}^{\top}\big) W_{\mathrm{down}}^{\top}$ \Comment{expand to 15360, gate, shrink back to 3840}
\State $h \gets r + \op{RMSNorm}(m)$ \Comment{post-feed-forward norm, then add residual}
\State \Return $h$
\end{algorithmic}
\end{algorithm}
\begin{algorithm}[H]
\caption{Block $\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{V}}(h)$ --- Vision encoder block}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\Require $h$ of shape $B\times T\times 1152$
\State $r \gets h$ \Comment{keep the input for the residual (skip) connection}
\State $u \gets \op{LN}(h)$ \Comment{normalise every token vector}
\Statex \hspace{-1.2em}\textit{token mixing: multi-head self-attention}
\State $Q \gets u W_Q^{\top} + b_Q$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}72$ (16 query heads)}
\State $K \gets u W_K^{\top} + b_K;\ V \gets u W_V^{\top} + b_V$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}72$ (16 key/value heads)}
\State $P \gets \op{softmax}(s\, Q K^{\top} + M)$ \Comment{mask: every patch/frame sees every other}
\State $O \gets P V$ \Comment{$B{\times}T{\times}16{\times}72$}
\State $m \gets O\, W_O^{\top} + b_O$ \Comment{concatenate heads, project back to $B{\times}T{\times}1152$}
\State $h \gets r + m$ \Comment{add residual}
\Statex \hspace{-1.2em}\textit{channel mixing: feed-forward network (each token independently)}
\State $r \gets h;\ u \gets \op{LN}(h)$
\State $m \gets \op{GELU}_{\tanh}(u W_1^{\top} + b_1) W_2^{\top} + b_2$ \Comment{expand to 4304, shrink back to 1152}
\State $h \gets r + m$ \Comment{add residual}
\State \Return $h$
\end{algorithmic}
\end{algorithm}

\section{What Each Layer Does}\label{sec:gloss}
One row per \emph{kind} of layer present in this model. ``Appears as'' gives the module names (layer indices removed).
\begin{longtable}{>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{5.4cm}>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{10.8cm}}
\toprule
Layer (appears as) & What it does \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
Layer (appears as) & What it does \\
\midrule
\endhead
\midrule \multicolumn{2}{r}{\textit{continued on next page}} \\
\endfoot
\bottomrule
\endlastfoot
\multicolumn{2}{l}{\textsc{Language model}} \\
\textbf{Token embedding}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{embed\_tokens}} & Lookup table with one learned vector per vocabulary token. Turns token ids into the vectors the network computes with (Gemma multiplies them by $\sqrt{d}$). \\
\textbf{Query projection (q\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}q\_proj}} & Turns each token vector into query vectors, one per head: ``what am I looking for?''. \\
\textbf{Query norm (q\_norm)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}q\_norm}} & RMSNorm applied to every query head vector before RoPE. Keeps attention logits in a sane range (QK-norm). \\
\textbf{Key projection (k\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}k\_proj}} & Turns each token into key vectors: ``what do I contain?''. Queries are compared with keys to decide where to attend. With grouped-query attention there are fewer key heads than query heads. \\
\textbf{Key norm (k\_norm)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}k\_norm}} & RMSNorm applied to every key head vector before RoPE. \\
\textbf{Value projection (v\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}v\_proj}} & Turns each token into value vectors: the information actually copied to tokens that attend to it. \\
\textbf{Output projection (o\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}o\_proj}} & Concatenates all heads' results and mixes them back into one hidden-size vector per token. \\
\textbf{Gate projection (gate\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{mlp.\allowbreak{}gate\_proj}} & Expands the token vector to the MLP width and passes it through SiLU/GELU. The result acts as a soft on/off switch for each hidden unit. \\
\textbf{Up projection (up\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{mlp.\allowbreak{}up\_proj}} & Expands the token vector to the MLP width (the ``content'' path). It is multiplied element-wise by the gate (SwiGLU/GeGLU). \\
\textbf{Down projection (down\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{mlp.\allowbreak{}down\_proj}} & Shrinks the gated hidden vector back to the model width so it can be added to the residual stream. \\
\textbf{Input norm (input\_layernorm)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{layers.\allowbreak{}input\_layernorm}} & RMSNorm applied to the residual stream before attention. Rescales each token vector to unit RMS, then multiplies by a learned per-channel weight. \\
\textbf{post\_attention\_layernorm}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{layers.\allowbreak{}post\_attention\_layernorm}} & RMSNorm applied to the attention output before it is added to the residual stream (Gemma ``sandwich'' norm). \\
\textbf{Pre-MLP norm (pre\_feedforward\_layernorm)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{layers.\allowbreak{}pre\_feedforward\_layernorm}} & RMSNorm on the residual stream before the MLP (Gemma). \\
\textbf{Post-MLP norm (post\_feedforward\_layernorm)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{layers.\allowbreak{}post\_feedforward\_layernorm}} & RMSNorm on the MLP output before it is added back (Gemma ``sandwich'' norm). \\
\textbf{Final norm}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{norm}} & RMSNorm applied once after the last decoder layer, just before the LM head. \\
\midrule \multicolumn{2}{l}{\textsc{Vision encoder}} \\
\textbf{Patch embedding (Conv2d)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{embeddings.\allowbreak{}patch\_embedding}} & A strided convolution that cuts the image into square patches (14$\times$14 pixels) and maps each patch to one vector. \\
\textbf{Position embedding}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{embeddings.\allowbreak{}position\_embedding}} & One learned vector per patch position, added so the encoder knows where each patch is. \\
\textbf{Pre-attention LayerNorm}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{layers.\allowbreak{}layer\_norm1}} & LayerNorm (mean/variance normalisation with scale and bias) before self-attention. \\
\textbf{Key projection (k\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}k\_proj}} & Turns each token into key vectors: ``what do I contain?''. Queries are compared with keys to decide where to attend. With grouped-query attention there are fewer key heads than query heads. \\
\textbf{Value projection (v\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}v\_proj}} & Turns each token into value vectors: the information actually copied to tokens that attend to it. \\
\textbf{Query projection (q\_proj)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}q\_proj}} & Turns each token vector into query vectors, one per head: ``what am I looking for?''. \\
\textbf{Attention output projection}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{self\_attn.\allowbreak{}out\_proj}} & Mixes the concatenated head outputs back to the model width. \\
\textbf{Pre-MLP LayerNorm}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{layers.\allowbreak{}layer\_norm2}} & LayerNorm before the MLP. \\
\textbf{MLP up-projection (fc1)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{mlp.\allowbreak{}fc1}} & Expands each vector to the wider MLP size before the GELU non-linearity. \\
\textbf{MLP down-projection (fc2)}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{mlp.\allowbreak{}fc2}} & Shrinks back to the model width. \\
\textbf{Final vision LayerNorm}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{vision\_tower.\allowbreak{}post\_layernorm}} & Normalises the vision encoder's output patches. \\
\midrule \multicolumn{2}{l}{\textsc{Multimodal projector}} \\
\textbf{Image-to-text projection}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{multi\_modal\_projector}} & Matrix mapping each pooled image feature (vision width) into the language model's hidden size. \\
\textbf{Projector norm}\newline{\footnotesize \texttt{multi\_modal\_projector.\allowbreak{}mm\_soft\_emb\_norm}} & RMSNorm on image features after average-pooling the 4096 patches down to 256 image tokens. \\
\end{longtable}
